# [Feat] [PPU] quantization: Support BF16 DeepGEMM for unquantized linear

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/46794) · state: closed · merged: False

## Original PR description



Refs #167

## Motivation

On PPU, unquantized BF16 linear layers currently fall back to `torch.nn.functional.linear` through `UnquantizedLinearMethod.apply()` and are dispatched to ACBLAS through ATen.

These BF16 dense GEMMs account for a significant share of Decode time in models such as Qwen4 and Qwen3.8-Flash-Next. This PR adds a PPU DeepGEMM path for BF16 dense linear operations and integrates it with the unified DeepGEMM JIT warmup flow, while preserving all existing fallback paths.

## Modifications

- Add a PPU BF16 branch to `UnquantizedLinearMethod.apply()`:
  - Call DeepGEMM when the input, weights, and optional bias are BF16.
  - Convert inputs and weights to contiguous tensors as required by `bf16_gemm_nt`.
  - Apply bias after the GEMM and restore the original output shape.
  - Preserve existing behavior for non-PPU devices, non-BF16 inputs, AITER, and CuTeDSL.
- Add `gemm_nt_bf16bf16bf16()` to `deep_gemm_wrapper/entrypoint.py`:
  - Invoke `deep_gemm.bf16_gemm_nt` through `deep_gemm_execution_hook()`.
- Extend `compile_utils.py`:
  - Add `GEMM_NT_BF16BF16BF16` without changing existing enum values.
  - Add a dedicated BF16 warmup executor.
  - Register the executor and add its warmup memory estimation.
  - Keep the existing `GEMM_NT_BF16BF16F32` and DSV4 FP32 paths unchanged.
- Add unit tests covering:
  - PPU DeepGEMM dispatch.
  - Non-PPU and non-BF16 fallbacks.
  - Warmup executor registration and memory estimation.
  - Entry point integration with the JIT execution hook.

## Accuracy Tests

Validation completed in the PPU v0.5.18 container:

- All 6 dispatch and warmup unit tests passed.
- Unified DeepGEMM warmup completed successfully on PPU-890P with `m_list=[28]`.
- The modified `UnquantizedLinearMethod.apply()` passed a numerical comparison against `F.linear` on PPU-890P with:
  - `M=28`
  - `N=1152`
  - `K=8192`

A full Qwen4 end-to-end inference accuracy run has not been performed yet.

## Speed Tests and Profiling

No end-to-end speed benchmark has been performed yet.

Follow-up benchmarking should compare ACBLAS and DeepGEMM on representative skinny Decode GEMM shapes, including GDN projections, QSA projections, HyperConnection, shared experts, and LM Head.
